[30/32] FORTE_WYCKOFF_ANATOMY_TRADING_RANGE: /market_books/wyckoff_anatomy_trading_range_forte
├── [TITLE] Jim Forte, "Anatomy of a Trading Range," MTA Journal, Summer-Fall 1994, pp. 47-58 · 12-page journal article, the Wyckoff-method trading-range layer · full text studied 18 Nov 2026 (third upload batch) · charts via Telescan 3.0 · bibliography: Hutson/Weis/Schroeder "Charting the Market" (TASC 1990), Pruden & Fraser Wyckoff Seminars (GGU 1992/93), SMI literature
├── [KEYWORDS] three Wyckoff laws as Forte states them | supply and demand | cause and effect | effort vs result | trading range = equilibrium zone | accumulation/distribution campaign = the cause | reward/risk >= 3 to 1 | Phases A-E | PS | SC | AR | ST | reaccumulation vs redistribution | Phase C = the test | spring/shakeout | SPRING-VOLUME TRICHOTOMY | point-and-figure count as the cause | the creek | JAC | SOS | LPS | BU | PSY | BC | SOW | LPSY | UTAD | the ice | distribution shorter than accumulation (UNVERIFIED flag) | SC does not confirm accumulation (the disclaimer)
├── [CORE] TR = equilibrium zone where professionals run accumulation or distribution campaigns; the campaign is the "cause" -> stated objective: enter in anticipation of the move out of a TR at a reward/risk of at least 3 to 1 | Law 2 operationalized by P&F counts: the count measures the cause and projects the effect | the schematics are ONE idealized model with many variations, and the presence of an SC does NOT confirm accumulation (it may be accumulation, redistribution, or nothing) - Forte's falsification honesty, rare and valuable, and the null-model discipline the programme's A2 engine needs
├── [MODELS]
│   ├── Accumulation Phase A (stopping the decline): PS pronounced buying after a prolonged downmove, volume/spread widen -> SC widening spread, panicky public selling absorbed by larger professional interests near the bottom -> AR selling exhausted, rally fueled by short covering, its high helps define the TR top -> ST revisits the SC area on significantly diminished volume and spread; SC/ST lows + AR high set the initial TR boundaries | honesty note: Phase A can end without climactic action, but dramatic selling "generally clear[s] out all the sellers," better for a sustained markup | reaccumulation caveat: a TR within an upmove has no PS/SC/ST and its Phase A looks like distribution's; Phases B-E analyzed the same
│   ├── Phase B (equilibrium, mixed clues): early = wide erratic swings, greater volume; late = diminishing volume as professionals absorb the last supply; support/resistance lines A/B/C/D contain price and frame the coming tests
│   ├── Phase C (THE decisive phase): either the stock starts leaving the TR with higher tops/bottoms, or a spring/shakeout breaks previous support - the shakeout preferred by Wyckoff logic (cleans out weak holders, creates a false directional impression, supplies dominant interests with additional stock from weak holders at low prices) | SPRING-VOLUME TRICHOTOMY (Forte's exact, encodable rule): supply surfacing on the break of support is (a) very light -> way clear for a sustained advance, (b) heavy -> usually renewed decline, (c) moderate -> more testing of support, proceed with caution | entries: point 8 (the shakeout itself, first prescribed long), point 10 (the test of the spring, better: a low-volume pullback with a specific danger point at 8)
│   ├── Phase D (markup begins): a consistent pattern of SOS advances (widening spread + increasing volume) and LPS reactions (smaller spread + diminished volume); if the pattern does not occur, do NOT add, consider closing the original position until evidence is conclusive | SOS brings the JAC (Jump Across the Creek, a deliberate break of the creek resistance on good spread/volume); adds at point 12 (BU back up to the creek edge / LPS after the JAC) and point 14 | Phase E: demand in control, setbacks short-lived, position management only | the creek: wavy resistance line across rally peaks inside the TR, minor and major creek lines crossed on the way up | P&F projections taken from points 8/10/12/14 project the extent of the move, set reward/risk before entry, later target close/adjustment areas
│   ├── Distribution (Schematics 2 and 3, inverse logic; "jumping the creek" -> "falling through the ice"): claimed asymmetry, distribution generally accomplished in a SHORTER time period than accumulation - encodable, UNVERIFIED | Phase A: PSY (pronounced resistance after an upmove) -> BC (urgent public buying filled by professionals near the top) -> AR (its low helps define the TR bottom) -> ST of the BC on diminished volume | redistribution = a TR within a larger downmove, Phase B-E analysis unchanged | Phase C: SOW (increased spread and volume downward) breaks the Phase B standoff, may or may not fall through the ice; the rally back to point 11 (LPSY) is unconvincing, narrow-spread, light or heavy volume (weak demand or substantial supply); first short at 11, better on the rally testing 11 (more evidence, tighter danger point) | UTAD alternative (Schematic 3): a pronounced move up through the TR highs that returns into the TR; signature = new highs on increasing volume with RELATIVE NARROWING spread, then return to the average level of closes of the TR = lack of solid demand = distribution, not accumulation; UTAD also strips weak shorts to dominant players and distributes to less-informed buyers before the decline | third variation (no schematic): upthrust with quick fall to the TR middle where the TR is too wide to be full distribution, concentrated selling absent except late in the TR | Phase D: supply dominance increases via the ice break or further SOW after an upthrust; shorts added at points 13/15/17 (LPSY rallies), pyramid only in profit | Phase E: markdown, supply in control, rallies feeble | the ice: wavy support line under reaction lows; break usually followed by attempts back above; failure to regain it may mean "drowning"
│   └── UT vs UTAD temporal distinction (Pruden's slides state it explicitly, Forte implies it): UT = minor penetration of the highs overcome by supply EARLIER in the TR; UTAD = the definitive terminal test LATE in the TR
├── [EVIDENCE] ZERO statistics anywhere in the article, stated as the note's front-loaded honesty flag: no counts, no nulls, no probabilities in the 12 pages; the evidence is annotated Telescan-era charts of one macro period each (a long-term accumulation with a Phase C light-volume break of TR lows then SOS on dramatically increased volume; two intermediate reaccumulations with springs/shake-outs on very light volume, no new supply, demand dominance off the spring; two distributions with PSY/BC on failing volume, SOW on increased volume, ice breaks, the MDT chart showing the UTAD following the right side of the TR then breakdown on significantly higher volume) | worked examples self-selected, n small per schematic | the article is a hypothesis generator with encodable definitions, NOT evidence
├── [EDGE] programme application: zero adoptions, zero status change, nothing tested against market data, the note's own mapping standing verbatim in intent: the full event set (PS/SC/AR/ST/spring/creek/JAC/SOS/LPS/BU and the distribution mirrors) each has an explicit price-volume definition in words -> parameterized detectors for the parked ORDERFLOW platform's Addendum A2 Wyckoff state machine, any evaluation its own new dated signed amendment | spring-volume trichotomy = a THREE-WAY pre-declared regime for the A2 spring detector (light -> hold, heavy -> reject, moderate -> wait) rather than a binary spring flag, Phase-5 H-W1 refinement context, Tier 1 (OHLCV + proxy volume) testable on decades of daily/weekly data | UT vs UTAD + the relative-narrowing-spread-with-rising-volume signature -> a parameterizable UTAD detector distinct from generic upthrusts, H-W2 context | distribution-shorter-than-accumulation = a cheap falsifiable Tier-1 claim (duration comparison across detected TRs, detector-lag caveat) | P&F count = the parameterized estimator of Law 2 (cause and effect): count the cause, realized move the effect, regression with block-bootstrap CIs per Addendum A2's Law-2 test | entry ladders 8/10/12/14 (long) and 11/13/15/17 (short): pre-registered add/exit ladders with the 3-to-1 reward/risk gate, pyramiding only in profit | SC != accumulation = the null-model discipline: event presence never confirms structure, only the full causal state sequence does | every item above is a candidate FUTURE hypothesis only, its own dated signed amendment required, nothing registered this session
├── [DISTINCTIONS] UT (early-TR minor penetration, overcome by supply) vs UTAD (late-TR definitive terminal test) | accumulation Phase A (with PS/SC/ST) vs reaccumulation Phase A (no climax, looks like distribution's, disambiguated only by larger context) | Dalton excess (low-volume terminal spike) vs Forte spring/shakeout (a breach whose volume trichotomy classifies it): both encodable, the volume sign carries the information | P&F count (Forte's cause estimator) vs the programme's estimator discipline: a pre-declared estimator only once frozen, otherwise another untested family member | schematics (one idealized model) vs live ranges (many variations): the disclaimer IS the null model
└── [+ADDED] full note standing in the market-studies KB as the source of truth (wyckoff-anatomy-trading-range-forte.md, 9090 bytes), this skeleton is routing only | cross-links in the note: Pruden 1997 (the Nine Buying/Selling Tests = the Phase C->D admission checklist, same SMI source), Williams/VSA (effort vs result instrumented), the Balan Elliott base (TRs as correction structures, accumulation/distribution as macro context), O'Hara and the microstructure stack (absorption = informed-flow mechanics behind "professional interests"), the ORDERFLOW platform spec (A2 event detectors, A6 Tier-1/Tier-2 two-tier design) | access honesty: full text, no unread depths, journal article studied complete on upload | registration bound honored at landing (session-201 hour): compression of the existing complete note ONLY, in this file's telegraphic format, zero new claims, zero new sources, zero status change, every figure already standing in the source note | the V-L1 extension moves to 3 of 5 (CMT Level III, Dalton, Forte landed; Pruden and Patel & Modi 2018 forward to their own hours)

# BTC-options unseen-ness candidate (DRAFT, UNSIGNED): the Deribit-lineage options event-structure readout on the signed Amendment 13 arm

Status: UNSIGNED DRAFT, zero status, never executed. No computation runs
before Andrea's dated signature, not even an entry-count check. The final
amendment number is assigned at signature in signing order (the standing
convention; Amendment 13 is signed, the fourteen label died with the refused
NDX draft and a new draft never inherits a refused number). This draft is the
Board-directed successor to the refused Amendment 14: at the 2026-11-19
directorate sitting Andrea refused the NDX/VXN options draft and directed a
BTC-options amendment instead, under the house pattern (pre-registration,
frozen defaults, power readout before sign-off, both paths recorded, own
citation audit of sources and availability limits). Self-critique attack
entry 4 (self_critique.log, this date) is this draft's entry ticket; its
bounding verdict is carried inside the sections below.

## 0. What this draft is and is not

It is a pre-registration of ONE descriptive readout: the event-structure
unseen-ness question of the unseen-ness layer, asked on the signed Amendment
13 (C-W1 BTC weekly) arm instead of the refused NDX route. It is not a
clock, not a WHEN claim, not a trade, it changes no Amendment 13 definition,
gate, kill line or forward protocol, and it adds no forward row to any log.
It is DEFINED on the Amendment 13 arm's own Stage A output and it dies with
that arm: if Amendment 13 closes at its occupancy gate or its design-stage
kill, this readout CLOSES NOT-APPLICABLE-ARM-CLOSED (pre-declared verdict
four below) and is never re-queued without a new dated amendment. It also
does NOT close the NQ/NDX event-structure question refused with A14: that
boundary stays unmeasured on the main asset; this draft answers the BTC
market's question only.

## 1. The question

The unseen-ness layer's unmeasured remainder (smh_unseen_v0 section 1c,
quoted verbatim in the refused A14 draft): whether the hazard's specific
event structure is priced. On the BTC arm this has never been asked at any
level: no BTC priced-ness cell (level, mean, event structure) exists in the
record. This draft pre-registers the event-structure cell only, before any
Deribit data is fetched: is the C-W1 BTC event (a >= 2.5 x sigma_w one-week
move within the 12-week horizon) priced in the BTC options surface at
Amendment 13 compression entries, on unsealed pre-2021 weeks only?

## 2. The readout, frozen before any data

Everything in this section is the refused A14 readout definition TRANSPLANTED
UNCHANGED to BTC by fixed rule (the Amendment 13 no-forking style), with the
yardstick and event drawn from the signed Amendment 13 construction. No
alternative window, tenor, band or convention may be computed alongside it.

- The sigma yardstick: the Amendment 13 sigma_w(t) (8-week trailing, ddof=1,
  weekly log close-to-close returns on completed UTC ISO weeks, the audited
  daily canonical). Never re-tuned.
- The event: the Amendment 13 frozen event, |r1| >= 2.5 x sig_w(t), first
  occurrence within 12 weeks, absolute value, direction refused.
- PRIMARY QUANTITY: the options market's own implied probability, at each
  Amendment 13 compression entry Friday, that BTC moves at least 2.5 x
  sig_w(t) within one week at some point in the next 12 weeks, extracted by
  the SOURCE'S OWN quoted convention (Breeden-Litzenberger state-price
  density where the source hands a full chain near the needed tenors; the
  source's own quoted implied-move convention where it does not). Never a
  convention built by this programme: the readout reads the crowd's prices.
- THE GAP: the median entry implied event probability minus the Amendment 13
  DESIGN cum12 (the value Stage A will record; it does not exist yet and this
  draft does not assume it). Matched climatology: the same implied
  probability at all pre-2021 Deribit-era decision weeks with a full forward
  12-week window (the C0 convention), median and Greenwood-style bootstrap
  band (B = 2000, block = 13 weeks, seed = 42, the house bootstrap
  constants), era-banded (2-year blocks, D-4 as a reporting note).
- SECONDARY QUANTITY (recorded, never headline): the term-structure slope at
  entries vs matched weeks (implied vol at the tenor covering 12 weeks minus
  the nearest liquid tenor, the source's own quoted units), so that a thin
  early-era chain still answers the curve-shape question.

PRE-DECLARED VERDICTS, exactly four, all descriptive:

1. PRICED: the entry implied-probability band overlaps the Amendment 13
   design cum12's Greenwood band. The event structure is priced on BTC too;
   recorded, no trading implication.
2. UNDERPRICED: the entry band lies wholly below the design band's lower
   bound. Recorded as a design-sample fact; licenses no trade, no rule
   change, no claim that the gap persists forward.
3. OVERPRICED: the entry band lies wholly above the design band's upper
   bound. Recorded; same descriptive status.
4. NOT-APPLICABLE-ARM-CLOSED: Amendment 13 closes at any of its own gates;
   this readout closes with it, without a number.

A single-direction band overlap is a NULL, recorded with both bands printed;
a null is uninformative in both directions (section 5). No second comparison
may be added after seeing the numbers; whatever looks interesting after the
fact stays descriptive, flagged, never trusted (the seductive-cell register).

## 3. Data sources and the citation audit (verified 2026-11-19, session 128)

- Deribit options history: the exchange launched 2016-07-14 (its own
  announcement) and its first BTC options traded November 2016 (the Deribit
  Insights four-years-of-volatility chapter). CONSEQUENCE, stated before any
  data is fetched: the design window 2014-09-17 to 2020-12-31 is only
  option-observable from December 2016 (roughly its final 4.1 of 6.3 years);
  compression entries before 2016-12-01 carry no quote BY FACT, counted in
  the denominator of the coverage gate as era-absent, never as missing data.
  Early-era (2016-2018) BTC option books were thin and early instruments are
  inverse (coin-margined, cash-settled, European) with sparse tenors; the
  coverage gate alone decides usability, at the gate's own risk.
- The free-default route: the Deribit public API v2, free access without a
  key for public market-data endpoints, including time-ranged historical
  trade endpoints whose historical trade records carry option implied
  volatility fields, with the exchange's own 2022 announcement that all
  historical trade and instrument data since launch is available through
  them. LIMIT, flagged honestly: these are TRADE records, not guaranteed
  end-of-day chain snapshots; an entry Friday whose nearest tenors have no
  nearby trades carries no usable quote under this route. The readout may
  therefore close UNTESTED-POOR-COVERAGE on the free route alone, and that
  closure is a correct recorded outcome, not a reason to buy anything.
- PRE-DECLINED, absolute: DVOL and every other Deribit volatility index
  (launched 2021-03-31 per the exchange announcement and press coverage) is
  a 2021+ series: it lies wholly inside the sealed window and NEVER enters
  any computation, descriptive or otherwise.
- The paid upgrade path, his word only, never the default: Tardis (the
  Deribit-lineage tick-data vendor the exchange itself partners with; its
  datasets include options data) and equivalent vendors (Amberdata,
  Laevitas). Coverage-start dates and free-tier terms UNVERIFIED this
  session (the vendor documentation was unreachable, erroring, at fetch
  time); any upgrade fetch re-runs this citation audit first. No data
  purchase is authorized by this draft; free routes first, the quality
  screen decides, his word upgrades (the platform GO convention).
- Provenance: any fetch enters docs/provenance_manifest.md first (fetch
  dates, endpoint or vendor, coverage, licensing note; never a licensed
  dataset into the repo).

## 4. Admission tests (in order, before any readout number)

1. DEPENDENCY: signed Amendment 13's Stage 0x and Stage A must have run and
   survived (verdict four otherwise). This draft never triggers them, never
   computes on their data, and never runs in the same session as either.
2. Provenance manifest entry (section 3).
3. COVERAGE GATE, fixed before any Deribit data was seen: at least 80
   percent of Deribit-era entries (entries on or after 2016-12-01), AND an
   absolute floor of 10 quote-carrying entries, must each carry a usable
   quote set at their entry Friday; else the readout CLOSES
   UNTESTED-POOR-COVERAGE (C-W3 and A14 occupancy precedent; the thresholds
   are frozen now, and any other pre-declared values would have been equally
   arbitrary).
4. Determinism: the full report byte-identical on a second run.
5. Look-ahead audit: every entry's implied probability recomputed from
   source data truncated at the entry Friday, exact match required (C-W2).
6. Sealed window: no 2021+ Deribit data enters anything; source files may
   span 2021+ by vendor design, but no 2021+ week, quote or series is read,
   described or counted, and DVOL is never read at all.

## 5. Power readout BEFORE sign-off

The effective n is bounded twice: by Amendment 13's occupancy (10-15
expected entries over the full window, fewer possible), and by the
Deribit-era restriction (entries after 2016-12-01 only). If entries fall
anywhere near the uniform rate, the option-observable subset may hold as few
as 7 to 10 entries, and each must also pass the coverage gate. At single-
digit-to-low-teens n, bootstrap and band widths are large: an
entry-vs-climatology difference in implied event probability smaller than
roughly 5 to 8 points is likely unreadable (the refused A14's VXN-analogue
honesty, transplanted; its n = 41 against roughly a thousand weeks is the
CEILING case, this arm's is below it on both sides). A null can neither
confirm nor refute unseen-ness; only a large mispricing is detectable. The
era restriction is itself a selection: the unobservable years 2014-2016
include materially different vol regimes, so the readout never claims
era-representativeness and reports the D-4 era banding. The decision this
draft asks of Andrea is whether the BTC question is worth asking at this
power ceiling; the draft takes no position.

## 6. Multiplicity, family, status

One pre-registered comparison, one cell, no grid; not a hazard claim, joins
no WHEN family, opens no clock, adds no forward row. The refused A14 stays
refused; closed verdicts stand. Its verdict, if any, is a design-sample
fact about BTC options pricing, recorded as the BTC arm's first priced-ness
cell (the event-structure cell; the BTC level and mean cells remain
unregistered separate questions, never bundled here).

## 7. What this draft cannot tell

Whether any pricing gap persists forward; exploitability after costs;
direction; microstructure (the L2 layer's question); anything about NQ/NDX
(the refused A14 market, whose event-structure boundary this draft does NOT
close); anything about any market other than BTC options; and anything at
all until Amendment 13's stages run, this draft is signed, the gates pass
and the numbers append.

## 8. Signature block

Unsigned. On Andrea's dated signature this becomes a signed amendment (its
number assigned in signing order); the admission-test results and the
readout append as numbered sections, never as rewrites. REFUSE: the BTC
event-structure question joins the NQ one as documented-not-actioned, and
nothing is lost from any proof object. Until his word, nothing in this file
has status and no tool may act on it.

### 2.1 Stage A design gaps (from the session-130 outcome record text)

The Stage A design gap on each arm, recorded as bookkeeping-never-proof:
BTC +0.3868 (the largest yet measured), NQ +0.193, GOLD +0.136, NIKKEI
+0.188, DAX +0.215. These five numbers are design bookkeeping only. A
gap is the difference between the arm's own design KM and its matched
climatology at design time; it is not evidence of an edge, it is a
measurement of the design's own internal contrast. No gap can be
promoted, re-tuned, re-worded, selected or compared across arms to license
anything. The full per-arm Stage A tables (entries, person-weeks, bands)
live in the arms' own amendments and are quoted only after re-reading:
docs/amendment13_cw1_btc_draft.md (BTC, the only one quoted below because
its figures are in the session-130 record text), and the NQ/GOLD/
NIKKEI/DAX amendment docs by name.

# Crypto order-flow claims ledger v0 (pre-registration, opened 12 Nov 2026)

New programme branch: crypto order-flow / liquidity research under adversarial falsification.
Governing prompt: Andrea's crypto master prompt (market-studies role: adversarial falsification;
WHEN / WHICH WAY / HOW MUCH separated; no direction from price-derived features alone; boundary
P(up)=0.505 [0.497,0.513] at n=15,386 from 64,887 weekly compression-state episodes, treated as
the standing direction boundary). Relationship to prior programme: this is a separate branch from
the NQ/SMH weekly programme. The NQ 2021+ holdout is spent and untouched; AVFI frozen constants
are never re-tuned; AVFI itself is out of scope for the order-flow layers (OHLCV proxy, the
governing prompt bans confusing it with microstructure). The parked ORDERFLOW platform spec (ofx,
Phases 0-9) is the engineering blueprint; this branch is its crypto-scoped opening. No evaluation
in this session: scoping, data audit, and claim pre-registration only.

## Single instrument first

BTCUSDT perpetual, Bybit derivatives (genuine volume, genuine L2, free public archive).
Everything else is refused until this instrument survives (governing REFUSE list: no unifying
theory before one-instrument survival). Panel pairs with synthetic volume (TradingView-contracted)
are banned from all volume features.

## Data audit (12 Nov 2026, verified by search; re-verify at acquisition time)

Held now: none. No crypto tick, trade, or L2 data exists in the knowledge folders.

Acquirable inside the research environment:
- Yahoo v8 route (proven in stage0c): daily and weekly OHLCV for crypto majors (BTC-USD, ETH-USD).
  Real exchange volume on majors. Gaps: no intraday via this route, no tick, no depth.

Free sources, acquisition must run on Andrea's machine (file sizes exceed environment fetch caps
of roughly 25MB; no websocket streaming here):
- Bybit public history archive (bybit.com/derivatives/en/history-data): orderbook files, JSONL
  with snapshot and delta records, up to 200 or 500 levels per side; publicTrade with aggressor
  side; klines; funding. Derivatives only. Coverage roughly 2023+ (about 2-3 years). This is the
  primary unblock candidate: tick-level L2 without paying.
- Binance Vision (data.binance.vision): klines (incl. 1m), trades, aggTrades (aggressor side
  derivable), bookTicker (best bid/ask with sizes). NO incremental full-depth diffs; futures has
  only aggregated depth snapshots. Restricted Layer 2 only: no cancellations, no depth beyond
  best level. Flagged proxy wherever used.
- Tardis.dev: incremental_book_L2 and trades CSV datasets; first day of each month downloadable
  without API key. Useful for engine cross-validation. Full history paid.
- OKX publishes L2 history; also reachable via CryptoHFTData free account with free hourly sample
  files. Secondary candidate only.
- LOBSTER: US equities only, not crypto. Ruled out.

Compute truth (this environment): Python available on upgraded sessions, pip installs forbidden,
stdlib only. hftbacktest, NautilusTrader, purgedcv are NOT installable. The event-driven replay
engine, purge/embargo, CPCV, DSR, and placebo machinery must be hand-built stdlib. House
reference for the engine acceptance test: the matching-engine framing from the Cont/Degond/Xuan
line (as recorded in the parked platform spec); the SMH programme already hand-built validated
bootstrap/surrogate machinery, so the precedent exists.

Acquisition plan shape: download on Andrea's machine, run the Layer 0 quality screen and feature
extraction there or in chunked passes, return aggregated feature tables / sampled windows into the
project for modelling. Raw multi-GB archives never need to enter this environment.

Data-quality screen (governing prompt, run BEFORE any modelling, per pair): fraction of book
updates that are cancellations vs trades; depth within 10bps of mid; autocorrelation of OFI.
Pairs with near-zero cancellation activity are excluded from absorption/Hawkes claims and the
exclusion is reported. A theory that works on 12 pairs out of 500 is a theory of a sub-population
and must be reported as one.

## Claims ledger

C-E1 (Layer 0, engine parity).
CLAIM: a reconstructed Bybit orderbook replayed from snapshot+delta records matches exchange
snapshots at 20 randomly chosen timestamps to within one tick.
MECHANISM: none; this is the pipeline's own admission test. Everything downstream is noise if it
fails.
TEST: reconstruct book from archive; compare mid and depth against venue snapshots at 20 random
timestamps; also cross-check one Tardis free first-of-month file.
KILL LINE: disagreement beyond one tick at any timestamp; then stop, fix, do not model.
STATUS: blocked-on-data (needs the Bybit archive download).
CANNOT TELL: whether the venue feed itself was correct; only replay fidelity.

C-W1 (Layer 3, WHEN, computable now).
CLAIM: after a pre-registered compression-range entry on BTC (daily and weekly, genuine-volume
data), the hazard of a volatility-scaled significant move within 12 bars exceeds matched
climatology.
MECHANISM: volatility persistence and mean reversion (GARCH-type clustering); compression
precedes expansion; duration dependence of the release.
TEST: entry-anchored hazard (KM / competing-risks, long format) vs matched-week climatology;
triple-barrier labels with vol-scaled barriers set at decision time; purge on label horizon with
embargo; persistence-preserving nulls (house style). Fresh windows only; no pooling with the NQ
programme.
KILL LINE: entry cum-hazard at 12 bars fails to clear the climatology band under the frozen
definition; forward kill lines at pre-declared row counts if promoted (house pattern 3/8/19/31).
STATUS: untested; computable with data acquirable through the existing OHLCV route.
CANNOT TELL: direction, causality, microstructure mechanism, post-cost exploitability, anything
about synthetic-volume pairs.

C-W2 (Layer 1, operationalization, computable now).
CLAIM: the phase layer's geometry components (ATR-scaled swing detection, Hurst classification,
range duration) are deterministic and computable at decision time from strictly prior data.
MECHANISM: none; reproducibility and look-ahead audit of the labels themselves (the REFUSE list
bans unoperationalized Wyckoff/Elliott labels; this claim is the operationalization).
TEST: implementation determinism; label-at-t uses only data timestamped < t; re-run equality.
KILL LINE: any label depends on data at or after its own timestamp, or two runs disagree.
STATUS: untested; computable now on OHLCV.
CANNOT TELL: whether the phases predict anything. Note: Layer 1's directional prior (signed OFI
inside the range, Kyle lambda on breaches) is blocked with C-D1; price-geometry direction is
refused by rule (the 0.505 boundary).

C-D1 (Layer 1/2 interface, WHICH WAY, blocked).
CLAIM: aggressor-side trade imbalance (taker buy vs sell) accumulated while price is flat inside
a range predicts the eventual breakout direction above the standing 0.505 boundary.
MECHANISM: absorption; one side's aggressive flow is fully absorbed by resting liquidity while
price holds (VSA effort-vs-result; Foucault/Pagano/Roell and Kyle lambda framings), and the
release goes the absorbed-against way.
TEST: triple-barrier direction labels, CPCV distribution of out-of-sample results, P(up) with
confidence interval; require lower CI above 0.505 on the single instrument first; trade-sign
placebo must return null.
KILL LINE: post-CPCV point estimate inside the boundary band or CI covering 0.50; placebo
non-null kills the pipeline rather than the claim, and is reported as such.
STATUS: blocked-on-data (aggTrades/publicTrade archive). PROXY FLAG: taker split has no depth and
no cancellations; absorption is inferred indirectly, not observed.
CANNOT TELL: the depth and cancel structure behind the absorption; queue dynamics; panel-level
generality.

C-M1 (Layer 2, best-level OFI, blocked).
CLAIM: best-level order-flow imbalance (bookTicker deltas on Binance, or level-1 orderbook deltas
on Bybit) predicts short-horizon signed mid-return beyond a trade-sign baseline, with the
Cont-Kukanov-Stoikov tautology controlled.
MECHANISM: liquidity redistribution at the touch (CKS; Bouchaud queue models).
TEST: short-horizon mid-return regressed on OFI vs trade imbalance; CKS event exclusion
(price-mechanics events dropped); CPCV; DSR with effective trials.
KILL LINE: no out-of-sample gain over the trade-sign baseline, or the effect dies under the
tautology control.
STATUS: blocked-on-data.
CANNOT TELL: depth beyond the touch; cancellations; whether the short-horizon effect scales up to
the daily/weekly event clock.

C-M2 (Layer 2, full depth, blocked).
CLAIM: multi-level OFI, cancellation imbalance, queue concentration, VPIN, and a Hawkes
excitation matrix over buy trades, sell trades, bid cancels, ask cancels carry incremental
information about event timing (hazard covariates) and absorption.
MECHANISM: liquidity withdrawal and self-excitation cluster events; absorption shows as
buy-to-buy vs sell-to-sell asymmetry while price is flat.
TEST: admitted only after C-E1 passes; Cox competing-risks with time-varying covariates; OFI-VPIN
correlation screen (above 0.8 means one layer measured twice, reported as such); proportional
hazards test, stratify on failure.
KILL LINE: per-feature lines declared in a dated amendment when the data exists. Standing line:
any feature whose shuffled-sign placebo returns non-trivial results is dead by construction.
STATUS: blocked-on-data (needs Bybit orderbook archive).
CANNOT TELL: anything until C-E1 passes.

C-V1 (validation machinery, stdlib, buildable).
CLAIM: the hand-built pipeline (purge/embargo on label horizon, CPCV, DSR with effective trials,
trade-sign placebo) reproduces its declared false-positive rate on placebo runs.
MECHANISM: none; pipeline self-control.
TEST: shuffled trade signs preserving book timestamps and depth; re-run; false-positive rate vs
declared tolerance.
KILL LINE: placebo rejection rate outside declared tolerance.
STATUS: untested; buildable once first trade data arrives. hftbacktest/NautilusTrader/purgedcv
not installable here; all machinery stdlib.
CANNOT TELL: anything about the market; only about the pipeline.

C-P1 (panel extension, protocol-refused).
CLAIM: none yet. Panel claims are refused until the single instrument survives.
STATUS: blocked-by-protocol-order. Group-aware purging is mandatory for any future panel work
(crypto pairs share a common factor). Volume features stay banned on synthetic-volume pairs;
which pairs were excluded and why must be reported.
KILL LINE: standing; any panel claim made before single-instrument survival is dead on arrival.

## Blocked layer and unblock paths

Blocked: Layer 0 and Layer 2 (the order-flow core), blocked on tick/L2 acquisition.
Unblock, ranked: (1) Bybit public orderbook+trades archive, free, tick-level, 200/500-level
snapshot+delta, derivatives, about 2023+; download runs on Andrea's machine. (2) Binance Vision
aggTrades + bookTicker, free, restricted Layer 2 (no cancels, best level only), flagged proxy.

websocket depth on Andrea's machine going forward (impossible in this environment).

## Governance

This branch does not touch: the spent NQ holdout, AVFI frozen constants, the H8/H9 nulls, the
SMH forward protocol, or the Sunday cadence. Any evaluation on this branch starts only after a
dated amendment fixing instrument, windows, definitions, and kill lines. Present status:

## Claims ledger

C-E1 (Layer 0, engine parity). UNCHANGED from v0. Blocked-on-data. Admission test

hazard vs matched climatology, daily and weekly, genuine-volume data, untested.

family claim under max-t.

C-W2 (Layer 1, operationalization, computable now). UNCHANGED from v0.

C-W3 (Layer 3, WHEN, REGISTERED 2026-11-14 under signed Amendment 12).
CLAIM: after a primed-state entry on BTCUSDT (daily bars, genuine-volume route),
the hazard of a 4-sigma barrier touch within 60 calendar days exceeds matched
climatology.
STATE (daily grain, verbatim from the v4 draft spec, untranslated): ATR(30) daily;
rvol_pct = prior-bar percentile rank of ATR_d over the trailing 52 daily bars;
comp_days = consecutive days with rvol_pct <= 33; range_pos = (close - min(low,
365d)) / (max(high, 365d) - min(low, 365d)); PRIMED ENTRY = first day with
comp_days >= 20 AND rvol_pct <= 33 AND range_pos <= 0.35. Strictly prior data;
determinism and look-ahead audit are the arm's Stage 0x admission test.

##FILE-BEGIN## trading-research/pipeline/docs/data_dictionary.md (4207 bytes)
# Data Dictionary

Status: normative reference for every CSV in `data/`. Last verified 2026-11-11 against the scripts named per file (scripts are the normative definition; this dictionary describes them and must be regenerated if a script changes).

## nq_weekly_canonical.csv

Normative script: `stage0c_canonical.py` (run via `research/verify_all.sh` or `research/stage0c_wrapper.sh`).

Weekly W-FRI aggregation of Yahoo v8 chart daily bars for the NQ front-month continuous contract (`NQ=F`, no adjustment). One row per exchange week. 1365 bars in the 2026-11-11 fetch.

| Column | Type | Definition |
|---|---|---|
| date | ISO date | Equals `week_end` (kept for downstream tooling). |
| week_end | ISO date | Friday (or last session day) ending the W-FRI week. Weeks with holidays end earlier; check `n_sessions`. |
| open | float | First daily open of the week. |
| high | float | Maximum daily high of the week. |
| low | float | Minimum daily low of the week. |
| close | float | Last daily close of the week. |
| volume | int/float | Sum of daily volumes, as supplied by Yahoo. |
| n_sessions | int | Count of daily bars in the week (5 normal, 4 on holiday weeks, fewer in the forming partial week). |
| thin_vol_flag | int | 1 if weekly volume < 0.25 x median of the previous 8 weeks (shifted one week, `min_periods=4`); else 0. Forced 0 for forming weeks dated beyond today + 7 days. |

Policies:
- 43 null daily rows dropped; 22 days with inconsistent highs/lows repaired by clipping close into [low, high]. Each repair is itemised in `research/stage0c_report.json` (`repairs[]`).
- The last row may be a FORMING partial week. Completed weeks only may drive decisions; the snapshot and the stage7 resolver enforce this.
- Fetch-staleness guard: if the last completed daily bar is more than 4 calendar days old, the pipeline errors rather than serving stale data.

## avfi_series.csv

Normative script: `strategies/avfi_replication.py`. Consumers: stage1 (signals), stage3, stage6b/c, stage7. Default parameters: hlVol 30, hlFlow 65, k 1, deadZone 0.2, gamma 2.5, smooth 3, warmup 260, emaLen 105, normWin 500, bandK 1.5. Sample stdev convention: `ddof=1` is THE programme convention.

| Column | Definition |
|---|---|
| close, volume | Pass-through from canonical. |
| sigma3 | Parkinson range volatility, exponentially smoothed (halflife 30). |
| z3 | Sigma z-score used for the vol-regime term. |
| w3 | Volume-surprise weight term. |
| rho3 | Signed return/vol association term. |
| flow3 | Signed flow estimate (contracts). |
| avfi3 | v3 composite AVFI indicator. |
| avfi2 | Legacy v2 indicator (retained for comparison). |
| emaV2 | Log-volume baseline EMA (lagged), the AVFI normaliser. |
| spread | avfi3 − emaV2. |
| sp_z | Spread z-score over rolling window `normWin=500`. |
| state | Discrete regime state (4-state labelling). |

Warmup: all derived columns are NaN until bar index >= 260.

## avfi_v32_tb_series.csv

Normative scripts: `strategies/avfi_tb_layer.py`, `research/stage1b_v32_replication.py`. Tops/bottoms measurement layer (v3.2): spread statistics (med_s, q25_s, q75_s, rsig_s, sp_rz), 20-week newHigh/newLow, divTop/divBot divergence flags, climax flags, cPos/cNeg consecutive-run counts, cusumAlarm, nearBreak, tbFlagged.

## avfi_v34_series.csv

Normative scripts: `strategies/avfi_v34_layer.py`, `research/stage1b_v34_replication.py`. Measurement layer (v3.4): range vol (sigma_r), Parkinson (sigma_pk), Garman-Klass (sigma_gk) plus smoothed variants, close location value (clv) and CLV-based flow variants (avfi3_clv, spread_clv, sp_z_clv), up/down vol decomposition (sigma_up, sigma_dn), asymmetry (asym).

## avfi_pgt_series.csv

Normative scripts: `strategies/avfi_pgt_layer.py`, `research/stage1b_pgt_replication.py`. Plane-geometry terminal detector (v3.5, Amendment 5 DRAFT, unsigned): g1Top/g1Bot, g2Top/g2Bot, g3Top/g3Bot event flags and pgtTop/pgtBot composite. Occupancy-only use until Amendment 5 is signed; must not drive entries.

## h3_wave_annotations_DRAFT.csv

DRAFT and UNSIGNED, with PENDING-ANDREA fields. Wave-count event set per `docs/h3_annotation_spec.md`. H3 is parked; no test may consume this file without a signed dated amendment.

##FILE-BEGIN## trading-research/pipeline/docs/frontier.md (14365 bytes)
# FRONTIER: The Living Research Matrix

Created 2026-11-19 under the Sovereign Agent Protocol. This file is the
LIVE ranking layer: which open vectors exist, their state, and their law.
The append-only machinery lives where it has always lived:
smh_frontier_v0.md keeps the gate list, the front register, and the
append-only frontier log (claims, outcomes, readouts); KNOWLEDGE.md keeps
the programme record. Rankings and vector states are maintained here, dated.
The ranking may be re-proposed autonomously by any session WITH recorded
reasoning in the frontier log; the owner's word at the directorate overrides
any autonomous re-ranking.

Registration rule (from the Protocol): every NEW vector must first survive
an attack entry in self_critique.log. No attack entry, no registration. The
attack is written by the proposing session itself and must argue honestly
that the vector is a waste of compute. The attack failing is the entry
ticket, not proof of value.

## The law for every vector

Every vector inherits the NORTH_STAR gates in full. Data/compute vectors run
only on unsealed data. Epistemic vectors add knowledge but zero status.
Infrastructure vectors never change a frozen quantity. Nothing here may
arm, ratify, move a kill line, touch a sealed window, or trade.

## The matrix (ranked, 2026-11-19)

V1 (F1, ACTIVE, recurring) Forward-row production and track health: the
Sunday cadence (stage 7), the SMH forward protocol, the macro Amendment 9
Sunday booking (first fire 2026-11-22 23:45 Malta). Feeds: the proof object.
Death: none. This front is the programme.

V2 (F7 line 1, ACTIVE, highest-priority new work) The BTC-options
unseen-ness amendment draft: the owner refused A14 (NDX/VXN/broker) and
directed a Deribit-lineage BTC-options draft under the house pattern
(pre-registration, frozen defaults, power readout BEFORE sign-off, both
paths recorded, citation audit of sources and availability limits).
Zero status until authored, audited, and signed.

V3 (F6, ACTIVE since the standing order signature) The generative
self-audit layer: pre-declared audits of already-registered machinery on
unsealed pre-2021 data (one knob, all cells reported, none selected),
first pre-declared in the frontier log BEFORE any computation.
Death rule: a selecting audit dies; three dead audits close the front.

V4 (new, registered this session) Signed Amendment 13 execution: C-W1 BTC
weekly Stage 0x as its own dated session (data audits, LOW_BTC by the rank
transfer, the occupancy gate, Stage A bookkeeping, kill lines frozen from
the arm's own design), then forward rows at the Sunday cadence.

V5 (F2, ACTIVE, recurring) Stewardship and verification: rebuild, verify_all
(25 checks), integrity, the two-tree sync. Feeds: the record's evidential
value.

V6 (F3, ACTIVE, standing rule) Website honesty pass: the site never lags the
record tail by more than one entry (sessions 121-124 rows currently owed to
the next demand-triggered class-U pass).

V7 (standing epistemic family) Estimator-discipline and base-rate audits:
the misses curriculum base rates, estimator-stability checks, the C8
macro-synthesis skeleton (assembles only when enough fronts carry honest
measured answers). Epistemic vectors here add zero status by construction.

V8 (F8, BLOCKED, keep-ready) Data-unlock preparation: L2 runbook, the
broker/CME 1999-2000 export pairing (A10), TradingView paste cross-checks.
Unblocks on the owner's hands.

V9 (infrastructure family) Meta-tool compounding (Protocol Phase 3): every
new script or statistical test is abstracted into a reusable function under
src/utils/ (first two mandated: calculate_base_rate(asset, window);
test_estimator_stability()), test-covered on the next Python-enabled session
and wired into verify_all chains where they are load-bearing. No unverified
code enters the chain.

## The swarm selection (Protocol Phase 2)

Each scheduled session selects up to three INDEPENDENT vectors from this
matrix, one per family where lawful: a data/compute vector, an epistemic
vector, an infrastructure vector; synthesizes all results into ONE record
entry; and appends per-vector lines to the frontier log. Three vectors is a
ceiling, not a quota: an honest single-vector session outranks a forced

##FILE-BEGIN## trading-research/pipeline/docs/h3_annotation_spec.md (2294 bytes)
# H3 wave-annotation data specification (for Andrea)

Rule set B (AVFI divergence at Elliott wave terminals, H3) is blocked until
historical wave annotations exist. This file defines exactly what is needed
so the effort is one pass over your old weekly charts, nothing more.

## What to record

One row per CANDIDATE TERMINAL you actually counted at the time, on the NQ
weekly chart, as far back as your annotations go (2007 or earlier is ideal;
2014+ alone is usable but thinner).

| field | meaning |
|---|---|
| wave_date | week-ending date of the annotated terminal (Friday) |
| wave_label | your label, e.g. "5 of A", "C of 4" |
| degree | your call: cycle / primary / intermediate / minor |
| direction | top or bottom |
| invalidation_level | the price level that killed the count, as recorded then |
| annotated_at | when you actually drew it (week-ending date of the session where the count was made) |
| hindsight_flag | "live" if drawn before resolution, "review" if drawn later from old charts |
| notes | optional context (overlap, alternation, channel, whatever you used) |

## Rules that keep H3 honest

- Prefer LIVE annotations from your existing weekly briefs over
  reconstructions. If you re-derive old counts from charts today, mark them
  "review": they are still usable, but the analysis reports live and review
  sets separately and never pools them for a pass/fail.
- Include the counts that FAILED (price ran through your invalidation).
  Terminals that were never annotated because they were obvious in
  hindsight are exactly the observer bias H3 must confront.
- No minimum count quality: send ambiguous ones too, flagged in notes.

## What happens once the file arrives

1. The event set is frozen and logged (no additions after testing starts).
2. H3 is tested as registered: conditional reversal frequency at terminals
   with vs without AVFI spread divergence (spread fading against the
   extreme, tolerance band pre-set before looking at outcomes).
3. Divergence definition, tolerances, and the pass threshold are
   pre-registered in an amendment BEFORE any outcome is computed.

Format: CSV (UTF-8, comma) or a simple table pasted as text. Dates as
YYYY-MM-DD. Anything unclear is better sent imperfect than not sent; the
pipeline asks rather than assumes.

##FILE-END## trading-research/pipeline/docs/h3_annotation_spec.md

# H3 draft extraction notes (2026-11-11, from the re-uploaded TradingView captures)

## What was uploaded and what it is

Seven captures: Gold weekly x2 (IMG_0116, IMG_0381), Gold monthly (IMG_0375),
Nikkei weekly (IMG_0374), Nikkei monthly (IMG_0378), DAX weekly (IMG_0379),
NDX weekly (IMG_0376). The NQ weekly capture used for the website chart room
is NOT in this batch; the NDX weekly is the only Nasdaq-market chart present,
so the H3 skeleton was drafted from it. NDX is the same market as NQ
(stage 0d: corr 0.982, divergences all explained), and week-ending dates
transfer directly, but the labels are his NDX count, not his NQ count.

## Method

Four vision passes over IMG_0376 (identify, historical-only, projected-only,
recent-structure). Passes were consistent on the major-degree skeleton
(2009 low, 2011 top, ~2012 low, 2015 top, 2016 low, 2018 top, pullback low,
Feb 2020 top, Mar 2020 low, Nov 2021 top at 16,465 which is the exact NDX
weekly close of 2021-11-19, 2022 low) and inconsistent on label texts,
degrees, and everything after 2022 (one pass produced prices incompatible
with actual NDX history; the minor-degree 2023-2026 labels and the projected
labels were unreliable and are NOT in the draft). The skeleton dates were
aligned to exact weeks and closes of the canonical NQ weekly series
(data/h3_wave_annotations_DRAFT.csv).

## What the draft is and is not

The draft is a PROPOSAL for Andrea's confirmation, not a frozen event set.
Per the H3 spec: the event set is frozen only when he signs it; the pipeline
asks rather than assumes. Fields only he can supply are marked PENDING-ANDREA:
invalidation_level (as recorded then), annotated_at (when the count was
actually drawn), hindsight_flag (live vs review; anything drawn on a chart
whose last candle is 2026-11-11 is "review" at best, but earlier-drawn
portions may be live), degree, and the exact label texts. Ambiguous terminals
(2012 low, 2015 top, 2018 pullback low) carry both candidate weeks in the
notes and must be resolved by him.

## Governance constraints on H3

- The 2021+ holdout is SPENT. Terminals 2021-11-19 and 2022-10-14 are in
  the spent span: recorded descriptively only, never evaluated. The 2023-2026
  structure ((5)/A/B/C and the live position) once provided would be logged
  the same way.
- Terminals on or before 2020-12-31 are on unsealed data and testable under
  the same regime as the executed H8/H9 studies: a new dated amendment
  pre-registering the divergence definition, tolerance band, and pass
  threshold BEFORE any outcome is computed, then a single execution.
- The H8/H9 nulls (divergence fades at fresh extremes without wave labels
  found nothing) do not pre-test H3: H3 is divergence AT his annotated
  terminals, a different event set. But the family relationship should be
  counted in the new amendment's multiplicity accounting.
- If his NQ weekly count differs from his NDX count, the NQ chart governs;
  re-upload it and the draft is rebuilt.

## What is needed from Andrea to freeze the H3 event set

1. Confirm or correct each draft row: label text, degree, direction, week.
2. For each row: invalidation_level as recorded then, annotated_at, and
   live vs review.
3. Add the terminals the extraction could not read: the 2023-2026 structure
   and anything on the chart left of the 2009 low.
4. State whether the NDX count equals his NQ count or the NQ capture should
   be re-uploaded.
5. Sign-off freezes the file (log-only for post-2020 terminals) and unlocks
   the H3 amendment drafting.

##FILE-BEGIN## trading-research/pipeline/docs/l2_setup_runbook.md (2884 bytes)
# L2 environment setup runbook (Andrea's machine; Phase 2 unblock)

Purpose: unblock the L2 layer (C-E1 first, everything else after). Written
2026-11-16. The ranked unblock path (Bybit primary, Tardis cross-check) is
frozen in the claims ledger; this is the operational version.

## 1. Engine

- Install Python 3.11+ on your machine. Then: pip install hftbacktest
  (wheels exist for win/mac/linux; no Rust toolchain needed unless you build
  from source). Project: github.com/nkaz001/hftbacktest.
- hftbacktest models queue position and latencies on L2/L3 tick data and
  ships Binance and Bybit examples. It does NOT excuse C-E1: parity within
  one tick at 20 random timestamps against venue snapshots is still the
  admission test, on the exact files you will use.

## 2. Data (primary: Bybit public archive)

- Page: bybit.com/derivatives/en/history-data. BTCUSDT perpetual
  (derivatives, linear).
- Download per month: orderbook files (JSONL, snapshot + delta records,
  up to 200 or 500 levels per side) and trading (publicTrade, aggressor side
  included). Coverage starts roughly 2023.
- Expect large files: a month of BTCUSDT orderbook data can be several GB
  compressed. Budget disk accordingly (hundreds of GB if you take the full
  2023-2026 span; start with 3-6 months to validate the pipeline, then extend).
- Keep the zips; convert to hftbacktest's npz format on the fly.

## 3. Cross-check (engine validation)

- Tardis.dev: first day of each month is free without an API key
  (incremental_book_L2 CSVs). Take a few BTCUSDT-perp days you also have from
  Bybit and confirm the reconstructed book agrees at matching timestamps.

## 4. Acceptance test (C-E1, from the ledger)

- Reconstruct the book from your archive; at 20 randomly chosen timestamps
  compare mid and depth against the venue snapshot records. Kill line: any
  disagreement beyond one tick. If it fails: stop, fix, model nothing.
- Record the pass/fail and the file list you tested; the record entry is the
  evidence, not the tool's own test suite.

## 5. What to bring back to the project

- NOT the raw archives (too big for this side). Bring back: the C-E1 pass
  record, plus aggregated feature tables (e.g. per-minute or per-event OFI,
  depth-within-10bps, cancellation fractions) sampled over windows we
  pre-register, so the L2 claims (C-M1, C-M2) can be evaluated with the
  programme's own validation machinery on this side.
- Before any modelling: the data-quality screen (cancellation fraction,
  depth within 10bps, OFI autocorrelation) per the ledger, reported honestly.

## 6. Governance reminders

- No evaluation on L2 data before a dated signed amendment for the specific
  claim. C-E1 itself is already registered; it needs no new amendment, only
  execution and an honest record.
- Nothing on the L2 branch touches the NQ programme, the macro arms, or any
  frozen constant.

##FILE-END## trading-research/pipeline/docs/l2_setup_runbook.md

##FILE-BEGIN## trading-research/pipeline/docs/lessons_learned.md (4255 bytes)
# Lessons from the AVFI-NQ programme (Nov 2026)

Written for the research site and for Andrea. These are the lessons the
programme's own evidence forced, not opinions.

1. The indicator was the least important part. AVFI v3, the spread axis, the
   z-scores: 27 parameter combinations and every variant of the entry logic
   failed, individually and as a family (White reality check p=0.11). The only
   thing that survived was a 12-week momentum rule anyone could write in one
   line. Months of indicator craft produced a regime descriptor; a one-line
   trend rule produced all the measurable edge.

2. Controls or it did not happen. The state system looked fine in validation
   (Sharpe 0.42) until the random-entry control scored 0.465 on the same
   window. Against the right control, an apparent edge evaporates. Buy-hold
   (1.03) and matched random entries are now mandatory in every evaluation.

3. One-episode edges are seductive. Bear-capitulation showed +0.23 sigma
   next-week, p=0.03, a textbook bounce. All 90 of its weeks were one
   2008-2010 episode. Statistical significance from one regime is not
   evidence of a repeatable edge; it is a description of history.

4. Elegant conditions can be structurally vacuous. The pre-registered
   |sp_z|>=1 transition filter produced zero trades ever, because state
   transitions happen exactly when the spread is near zero, hence sp_z near
   zero. A filter that never fires is not conservative, it is dead code. Test
   whether a rule can trade at all before testing whether it wins.

5. Costs were not the enemy. At the weekly horizon, tripling slippage moved
   Sharpe by less than 0.012. The enemy was absence of edge, not friction.
   Cost paranoia is misplaced at this holding period; edge paranoia is not.

6. Parameter sensitivity usually hides a slowness artifact. The H5 scan
   looked like a plateau (best 0.63 at slow EMA settings) but decomposed into
   a pure slow-EMA drift effect. When a parameter "works" only at extreme
   values, ask what single simple thing it is proxying before celebrating.

7. Data discipline precedes strategy discipline. The canonical series was
   built before any rule was tested: repairs logged one by one, cross-checked
   against the cash index (correlation 0.982, every divergence week explained
   by roll windows or documented crises), single-source risk quantified
   instead of ignored. Bad data produces confident nonsense.

8. Reproducibility is insurance. Six environment resets, six identical
   rebuilds. Every number in this programme has been recomputed from raw data
   multiple times with the same seed and the same answer. If a result cannot
   survive being rebuilt, it was never a result.

9. Power analysis before the irreversible decision. The holdout is a one-way
   door, so before touching it: the battery passes at observed Sharpe ~0.75
   over 307 weeks, meaning a true Sharpe-0.57 edge clears the bar only 43% of
   the time. Knowing the odds of each outcome before spending the sample is
   the difference between testing and gambling.

10. Entries got all the attention; sizing was the untouched lever. Stage 4c:
    keeping the identical 12-week signal and varying only position size
    (vol targeting plus a regime throttle) improved drawdown from -21.5% to
    -14.2% with a slightly better Sharpe. The next real improvements in this
    programme will come from how much, not when.

11. Within-sample resampling cannot test smooth weekly series. The
    Amendment 4 power readout measured it: the registered sp_z series has
    lag-1 autocorrelation 0.985 (persistence scale ~67 weeks, effective n
    around 2) and the two candidate predictors correlate 0.934. Under a
    within-realization bootstrap the true sampling variance of the
    correlation difference is ~2.2x the bootstrap's, so the as-drafted test
    had a 7-16% false-positive rate against a nominal 5% - and longer blocks
    (13/67/130/260) made it worse, not better, because the defect is
    persistence, not block length. Any correlation-style test on the smooth
    weekly series at n~300 needs a persistence-preserving null (circular
    shift of whole realizations) or event-study machinery, never resampling
    inside one realization.

##FILE-END## trading-research/pipeline/docs/lessons_learned.md

##FILE-BEGIN## trading-research/pipeline/docs/north_star.md (3025 bytes)
# NORTH_STAR: The Constitution of the SMH Programme

Ratified 2026-11-19 by the owner's directive (the Sovereign Agent Protocol,
SMH V2.0). Immutable: no session may edit this document; any change to it
requires Andrea's dated word, and the change is appended, never rewritten.

## The immutable goal

The creation of the Salient Market Hypothesis (SMH) as a first-of-market,
falsifiable theory of liquidity and time: recurring, measurable conditions
under which a significant market impulse becomes materially more likely,
stated so that it can fail in public.

The two load-bearing sentences, absolute and permanent:

The default assumption is NO EDGE. The burden of proof is absolute.

## What the goal means, precisely

1. A "first-of-market" theory is earned, not declared. The only earning
   instrument the programme recognizes is the forward calibration record:
   sealed, timestamped predictions scored as the market resolves them.
   Backtests, in-sample statistics, and visual configurations are bookkeeping,
   never proof.
2. A theory of liquidity and time must keep its layers separate: market-state
   measurement, event probability, any precursor, directional evidence, and
   execution context are distinct objects. Conflating them is how theories
   launder themselves.
3. No dated instance is ever "inevitable". Actuarial recurrence is the only
   inevitability the record supports (the SMH ECS v0 standing: occurrence
   0.634 at the compression cross vs 0.441 climatology; kill lines 3/8/19/31
   events; the positive proof is exactly 57 of 104 resolved windows).
4. When the math says a candidate is dead, the programme writes its obituary
   and moves on. The falsification record IS the asset.

## The inviolable gates (Phase 5 of the Protocol)

No session of any autonomy degree, in any lineage, may: fabricate or
backfill data; move a kill line; change a label definition to force a pass;
evaluate or descriptively read a sealed or spent window (NQ 2021+ SPENT;
GOLD/NIKKEI/DAX and BTC 2021+ sealed); arm, ratify or execute an amendment;
re-tune after a null; claim or score direction without forward evidence;
treat a backtest as evidence; execute or recommend a live trade; rewind or
rewrite record history. Unknown cases: refuse and log the refusal.

## Governance line

Andrea is the Board. The delegated sessions hold HOW and WHAT-next authority
within the lawful frontier, per the signed standing order and its 2026-11-19
addendum. Arming, kill lines, claim closing, sealed windows, and anything
that trades remain the Board's word forever. The CSO function is accountable
through the weekly DIRECTORATE_REPORT and the programme record.

Companion documents, all in this bundle: FRONTIER.md (the living research
matrix), self_critique.log (the pre-registration attack ledger),
sovereign_agent_protocol_v1.md (the installed protocol), directorate_report.md
(the weekly human interface), standing_order_autonomy.md (the signed
standing order), decision_queue_v0.md (the pending-decision stack).

##FILE-END## trading-research/pipeline/docs/north_star.md

##FILE-BEGIN## trading-research/pipeline/docs/platform_charter_v0.md (9814 bytes)
# ORDERFLOW platform charter v0 (the start document)

Dated: 2026-11-19. Status: IN FORCE on the owner's dated word. Andrea's
instruction in the main conversation, verbatim: "Ok continue in new
direction", then his selection of the offered direction "Start the
orderflow platform" (an explicit option pick, recorded as his GO for the
programme front, not as a signature on any specific amendment). The same
message supplied his autonomy framework text (task selection via a ranked
fronts list, standing order, weekly directorate, hard gates), already
shipped as docs/autonomy_charter_v1.md and this queue's protocol; the
platform GO is exactly the kind of standing-order directive that framework
licenses. This charter is the governing document for platform work under
the autonomy charter; where the two disagree, the autonomy charter and the
frozen record win.

## 1. What the platform is

The ORDERFLOW platform is the registered successor programme: a research
platform that (a) ingests and normalizes raw tick, trade and order-book
data of the formats actually provided, (b) reconstructs limit order books
where the data permits, (c) computes a documented, point-in-time-safe
order-flow feature library, (d) expresses hypotheses as declarative
pre-registered specifications, (e) tests them with realistic execution
simulation and costs, (f) validates with multiple-testing-aware
statistics, and (g) reports honestly, negative results included. It is
the execution vehicle for the crypto branch's registered claims
(C-E1, C-W1, C-W2, C-W3-closed, C-D1, C-M1, C-M2, C-V1, C-P1-refused in
docs/crypto_orderflow_claims_v2.md, the single authoritative claims
ledger) and for any future claim family admitted by dated amendment. The
standing default holds everywhere: there is no edge until the record
proves one; the burden of proof never moves.

## 2. Data design (frozen by the ledger and runbook)

Two tiers, never silently mixed:

  Tier 2 (true flow; Phase 2 unlock). Bybit public archive, BTCUSDT
  perpetual (derivatives, linear): orderbook JSONL snapshot+delta files
  (up to 200 or 500 levels per side) and publicTrade files (aggressor
  side included), coverage roughly 2023+, per docs/l2_setup_runbook.md.
  Engine: hftbacktest on the owner's machine. Cross-check: Tardis.dev
  first-of-month free files. The download is HIS hands-only item; the
  raw archives never enter this side. This side receives the C-E1 pass
  record plus aggregated feature tables over pre-registered windows.

  Tier 1 (proxy flow; computable now). Daily/weekly OHLCV (Yahoo from
  2014 for BTC) for design bookkeeping of the C-W family only. Tier 1
  numbers are bookkeeping, never proof; genuine-volume routes only
  (synthetic-volume pairs banned, C-P1).

## 3. Source registry and claims traceability (KB protocol, no drifting copies)

The knowledge base already satisfies the KB protocol in substance
(record, the quant-resources application). Two anti-drift decisions are
made here explicitly, per the session-114 mirror lesson (a copy nobody
reconciles drifts):

  KB-1, KB-5, KB-6 traceability: the source registry is NOT a duplicate
  file. A platform source id is the exact market-studies note filename
  or provenance-manifest hash it cites (for example
  orderflow-cont-kukanov-stoikov-2014-ofi.md). Every hypothesis, feature
  and cost assumption must cite its source file, or be labelled ORIGINAL
  EXPLORATORY IDEA, on the record.

  KB-2: the claims registry is the ledger itself
  (crypto_orderflow_claims_v2.md), not a second machine-readable copy.
  A claims.jsonl export is deferred until a machine consumer exists, and
  if ever created must ship with a currency check in the chain.

  KB-3 and KB-4: contradictions and gaps live in the ledger's honest
  sections and the record. Known gaps carried here: the Bouchaud impact
  and queue chapters (partial book, full text outstanding), Hawkes
  machinery (Amendment 10, data-blocked), and the un-fetched primary
  texts flagged in the market-studies notes. Nothing is filled from
  general knowledge without a NOT-FROM-KB label.

## 4. Phases and hard gates

Phase 0 (THIS SESSION, bookkeeping only; complete on shipment): this
charter, the standing-order mapping, the queue's platform items seeded.
No market data, no evaluation.

Phase 1 (autonomous sessions, SYNTHETIC DATA ONLY; lawful build class):
the ingestion and normalization layer for the two named archive formats
(Bybit orderbook/trade JSONL, Tardis incremental_book_L2 CSV), the LOB
reconstruction module, the data-quality screen (cancellation fraction,
depth within 10 bps, OFI autocorrelation, per the ledger), and the C-V1
validation machinery (stdlib). Every module ships with synthetic
fixtures and self-tests, executed and shown, in the stage1b tradition.
GATE: nothing in Phase 1 may touch market data; all tests synthetic.

Phase 2 (HIS DATA): the L2 branch clock starts only when his download
lands. C-E1 is the admission test for everything L2: book reconstruction
against 20 randomly chosen timestamps, kill line any disagreement beyond
one tick; stop, fix, model nothing on failure. The first L2 claim
evaluation additionally requires its dated C1-L2 registration amendment.
No evaluation on real L2 data before both.

Phase 3 (feature library, descriptive occupancy only until per-claim
amendments): best-level and integrated multi-level OFI (Cont/Kukanov/
Stoikov; Cont/Cucuringu/Zhang, depth-scaled, dimension reduction before
cross-impact), volume-clock sampling as the Lesson 11/13/15 fix for
near-integrated flow series (Easley/Lopez de Prado/O'Hara), footprint,
sweeps, icebergs, pace (the ofx families), VPIN only under its
contested-proxy caution. Macro-structure sublayers (ofx Addenda A/B):
the Wyckoff A2 state machine including the session-94 registered
encodable pair (Forte's TR anatomy, Pruden's Nine Tests) as Tier-1
descriptive candidates; the Elliott A3 enumeration engine ONLY behind
its mandatory null tests N1-N4 (pattern frequency vs random walks,
ratio clustering vs shuffled pivots, incremental value over controls);
the Katsanos intermarket tolerance-window regression divergence family as
a cross-market descriptive template (CMT ch 8-14 keyword bank, NO new
computation without amendment). Addendum B's 3D state space and
persistent-homology layer requires his explicit green light and stays
zero status (Nakahara verdict: methodological license only).

Phase 4 (pre-registrations): every hypothesis before any computation,
with null and sanity baselines, power readouts BEFORE sign-off, honest
trial counts, kill lines frozen from each arm's own design. The frozen
rule set of the house applies: family max-t for variants of one
indicator; SPA consistent p-values (all three bounds) for family
searches; BH with the BY correction for independent shortlists; DSR for
final picks; PBO/CSCV on raw per-period returns only; Theiler-style
persistence-preserving nulls for any correlation-style test on smooth
series; the regime-vs-breaks fork declared per hypothesis
(Diebold-Inoue vs Bai-Perron); per-period statistics only, annualized
headline Sharpe banned.

Phase 5 (execution simulation and costs): realistic fills, queue
position via hftbackest or equivalent on his machine, spread and impact
assumptions traced to their sources; post-cost exploitability reported
as a separate, later question, never blended into a timing claim.

Phase 6 (reporting): append-only record entries, negative results
reported, the website the honest face of the branch.

## 5. What this GO does NOT do

It signs nothing. Amendment 13 (C-W1) remains a signature decision; the
BTC 2021+ window stays sealed, single-open on his dated go; C-W3 stays
closed (its barrier-repair ratification still pending); no paid data
tier is authorized (free routes first, the quality screen decides, his
word upgrades); the NQ programme, the macro arms, the Sunday cadence,
the V4h track and every frozen constant stay untouched. The daily
autonomous task (first fire Friday 2026-11-20 09:00 Malta) may execute
Phase 1 build items under the autonomy charter's build class; it may
not evaluate, arm, ratify, or touch any window.

## 6. Book layer grounding (the layers this design rests on and their
status)

Supported by: Cont/Kukanov/Stoikov (linear OFI, tautology control as the

## 7. Kill criteria of this charter

His one word revokes or redirects it; a Phase 1 module that cannot pass
its synthetic tests by its third honest attempt closes that module's
track with the failure recorded; a C-E1 fail stops the L2 branch
outright; and any evaluation found on real data without its dated
amendment voids the platform's standing at his next read. No
self-certification: the platform's only admissible proof object is the
forward record, exactly as for SMH.

##FILE-BEGIN## trading-research/pipeline/docs/ruleset_v_spec.md (4689 bytes)
# Rule set V: the from-scratch synthesis strategy

Status: REGISTERED 2026-11-12 for stage 7 forward paper tracking, as the
third parallel row (T tracked, U3 parallel, V parallel). Written from
scratch on Andrea's explicit instruction ("rewrite from scratch a new
strategy now with all that you have done taking into consideration").

Evidence status, stated plainly:

  * V's components were chosen from findings measured on UNSEALED
    2000-2020 data (the record through stages 1-6b plus the structural
    scans). Any diagnostic run on 2000-2020 is therefore IN-SAMPLE
    DESIGN BOOKKEEPING, not confirmation. No p-value is computed or
    implied for V anywhere, and none may be.
  * The 2021+ holdout is SPENT. V is never evaluated on it, the same as
    every other rule. V's ONLY evaluation path is the stage 7 forward
    paper track under the same pre-committed standards as T and U3
    (26+ entries minimum, kill criteria, every week logged).
  * If a one-shot confirmatory unsealed test of V is ever wanted, it
    requires a NEW dated amendment signed by Andrea BEFORE execution,
    and it must use persistence-preserving machinery (lesson 13).
    No such amendment exists.

## The rule

Signal (identical to rule set T, the only replicated edge in the
programme):

  mom12 = log(close_t / close_{t-12}) at the Friday decision close.
  Long at next Monday open when mom12 > 0, flat otherwise.
  Signal exits only. Cost convention 0.3625 pts/side.

Position size when long (the part that is new):

  vol8 = 8-week realized vol of weekly log returns, annualized
         (sample stdev, ddof=1, the same convention as U3 and the
         validated Pine vol8 parity).
  tgt  = 0.15 if the AVFI state at the decision close is reversal_down,
         else 0.20.
  size = min(1.0, tgt / vol8) when long, 0 when flat.

Frozen constants (registered with the rule; never re-tuned):

  momLen 12, volWin 8, volTgt 0.20, defTgt 0.15, cap 1.0,
  cost 0.3625 pts/side. No leverage, no short side.

## Why each component is in V (and what is deliberately not)

1. Trend entry, 12-week momentum long/flat. Licenced by the single
   strongest result the programme owns: T's magnitude replicated almost
   exactly out of sample (0.571 unsealed, 0.575 holdout, p 0.069). Every
   AVFI entry variant died (27 of 27, jointly and individually). Lesson
   1: craft is not edge; the generic trend prior is the whole edge.

2. Volatility targeting to 20% annualized. Licenced by the one sizing
   property that partially HELD out of sample: U3's drawdown control
   (-13.0% vs T's -23.4% on the holdout). Lesson 10: sizing changes
   what you lose.

3. Graduated regime modulation of the TARGET (20% -> 15% in
   reversal_down) instead of U3's binary position halve. Licenced by
   lesson 12: U3's binary 0.5 throttle in reversal_down charged a third
   of the upside as premium across a holdout in which the payout never
   came. V keeps the insurance but re-prices it: a 25% target reduction
   in the risk state, stacking with the vol target's automatic
   shrinkage when volatility actually spikes (the insurance that
   always pays when it is needed, without a state bet). In reversal_down
   at typical vol8 (~23%), V sizes ~0.64 vs U3's ~0.42 - defensive, but
   no longer surrendering the trend's good weeks at a flat 50% rate.

Considered and deliberately REJECTED from V:

  * Binary state throttle (superseded by the modulation above).
  * Hamilton regime gating (stage 0g is descriptive; no forward
    evidence; untested machinery does not enter a registered rule).
  * The v3.4 measurement layers (GK floor, CLV factor, up/down
    asymmetry) as sizing or entry inputs - Amendment 4 withdrawn.
  * PGT plane-geometry flags - Amendment 5 withdrawn, display only.
  * Any |sp_z| filter - lesson 4: elegant conditions that never trade.
  * Short side / momentum_down states - extinct since 2010 (H7
    untestable).
  * Order-flow constructs (OFI, VPIN, optimal transport) - real ideas
    from the study programme, wrong universe: they live on order book
    data at intraday frequency; the registered universe is weekly bars.
  * Parameter adaptation to the Bai-Perron breaks (untested machinery;
    lessons 6 and 13).
  * Leverage.

## Execution and tracking

Identical machinery to T and U3: decision at the Friday close, fill at
next Monday open at cost, mark to close, append-only stage 7 log, paper
account only. V appears as a third parallel row on the weekly snapshot
card and the Sunday log; it books no paper position of its own until
Andrea promotes it (which would be a dated decision, recorded).

Registered by: programme session 21, 2026-11-12, on Andrea's instruction.

# SELF_CRITIQUE log (append-only; one attack per frontier proposal)

The rule (Sovereign Agent Protocol, Phase 1.3): before any new vector enters
FRONTIER.md, the proposing session writes a one-paragraph honest attack on
why that vector is a waste of compute. If the attack does not kill the
proposal, the vector is registered; the attack text is never edited after
being written. Attacks on dead or refused vectors are kept as the record of
why they died.

--- 

Entry 1, 2026-11-19, session 125. Object under attack: the Sovereign Agent
Protocol adoption itself (this architecture).

ATTACK: This protocol is ceremony displacing object-level work. The
programme already has a signed standing order, a frontier log, a decision
queue, a governance spine, and 25 green verification checks; installing a
second vocabulary (NORTH_STAR, FRONTIER, SWARM) adds governance surface
without adding one forward row, which is the only thing the programme
values. Worse, the swarm mandate multiplies load on the exact failure mode
the record already proves: concurrent sessions have clobbered each other's
writes repeatedly (sessions 8 and 17 record-repair notes), and 24 hourly
frontier sessions plus 24 hourly deep-reads already run daily; tripling
per-session workload multiplies collision probability and log bloat. The
meta-tool rule invites untested generic code into a chain whose entire value
is determinism.

VERDICT: the attack partially holds and the protocol was MODIFIED, not
adopted verbatim. The live matrix (frontier.md) replaces nothing; the
append-only log protocol stays in smh_frontier_v0.md unchanged, so the
collision discipline does not change. The swarm is three vectors as a
ceiling, synthesized into ONE entry, with the honest empty session still
valid, and only lawful fronts selectable. Meta-tools enter only through
Python-enabled sessions with tests and verify_all wiring. The naming layer
is adopted because the Board directs it and it names what already existed.
Entry 2-3 attacks were written for the same sitting's registrations.

---

Entry 2, 2026-11-19, session 125. Object under attack: V4, executing signed
Amendment 13 (C-W1 BTC weekly) Stage 0x.

ATTACK: This is compute spent on an arm the record's own precedent says may
close at the occupancy gate exactly as C-W3 closed (7 entries against the
gate of 10). The BTC weekly clock is a rank-transfer construction on a
consolidated-feed, price-only source; the sealed 2021+ window stays untouch-
able forever, so the only possible yield is forward rows that C-W3's curve
suggests may starve. It competes for the same Sunday cadence slots that the
NQ proof object needs, and a starved arm adds log volume with no evidence.

VERDICT: the attack fails to kill the vector. The amendment is SIGNED, its
occupancy gate is itself the pre-committed discipline (a closed arm is a
clean, cheap, recorded outcome, not a failure); forward rows from the
signature date are the programme's only proof path; and cost is one dated
Stage 0x session plus cadence lines. Registered as V4.

---

Entry 3, 2026-11-19, session 125. Object under attack: V9, the meta-tool
compounding family (src/utils/, Phase 3).

ATTACK: Generic abstractions written by scheduled sessions are exactly how
subtle divergence enters a chain whose numbers must reproduce byte-ident-
ically across resets; a calculate_base_rate(asset, window) helper that
silently differs from the frozen per-market scripts would corrupt every
downstream count, and test_estimator_stability() invites re-testing frozen
estimators, which brushes the no-re-tuning-after-null gate.

VERDICT: the attack holds in its risk and the vector is BOUNDED, not
killed. Meta-tools are new-family helpers only; frozen scripts are never
retroactively replaced by generic calls; every meta-tool lands on a
Python-enabled session with its own tests, and load-bearing uses are wired

nitions, and stability tests target machinery robustness, never re-tuning.
Registered as V9 under those bounds.

BTC-options unseen-ness amendment (the Board-directed successor to the

VERDICT: the attack fails to kill the vector and BOUNDS it. The Board's
